# All 3D assets on the machine

A survey of the 3D assets in four places, made 2026-11-09 by `asset_survey/all3d_survey.py` (run it again to bring this file up to date). [assets.md](assets.md) says which of them the levels use and credits them; this file lists everything that is there, with its path.

- **How it was read:** nothing was extracted or opened in an editor. The Fab packs from their own manifests (title, tags, store link, engine versions, file list), the CGAxis volumes from their zips' file lists and the catalogues beside them, the KitBash3D kits and the project's folders from their files on disk.
- **Counts** of skeletal meshes, static meshes, clips (animations), effects and the rest are counted from the asset names (`SK_`, `SM_`, `A_` or an Animations folder, `NS_`...): they are approximate, as each pack names things its own way.
- **Licences:** the Fab packs under the Fab licence (`I:\game assets\3d fab library data cache\FAB STANDARD LICENSE AGREEMENT.pdf`), CGAxis under its royalty-free licence (in a game, credit "models by CGAXIS.com"), the KitBash3D kits under KitBash3D's. None of them may be put in the repository.

## Contents

1. [The project](#1-the-project-ecodeclaude-ue5)
2. [CGAxis models](#2-cgaxis-models-igame-assets3d-cgaxis-models)
3. [The Fab library](#3-the-fab-library-igame-assets3d-fab-library-data-cache)
4. [KitBash3D kits](#4-kitbash3d-kits-cuserskestanolappdataroamingkitbash3dkits)

## 1. The project (`E:\code\claude-ue5`)

## 2. CGAxis models (`I:\game assets\3d cgaxis models`)

CGAxis's Complete collections 1-13 as zips (one zip per format for Complete 1-10, one zip per volume for 11-13). Realistic, high-polygon models made for architectural rendering (3ds Max, Cinema 4D, Maya and V-Ray scenes, with FBX and OBJ copies; Unreal assets from volume 101 on). None is game-ready: no LODs, no collision, and up to millions of polygons a scene. The models' files are numbered (`cgaxis_models_<volume>_<n>`), so a volume's subject is its title. A preview zip (one PDF a volume) or a catalogue PDF sits beside each set.

### The models volumes

Titles: cgaxis.com's product pages, the complete.cgaxis.com pages, the catalogue PDFs ("catalogue": named from the catalogue's description), the zips' names. Models: the volume's FBX files in its zips.

## 3. The Fab library (`I:\game assets\3d fab library data cache`)

The Epic Games Launcher's vault cache: each downloaded Fab pack in `VaultCache\<pack>` (its `manifest` and its `data\Content`), and the library's listings in `VaultCache\FabLibrary\<listing>\unreal-engine\manifest` (a listing's manifest names every file of the pack, downloaded or not). "In the project" names the `claude_ue5/Content` folder when the pack's folder is there.
